I just feel like I'm really not handling the change well, and I needed to talk to someone about it.\n\nDoctor: When exactly did you start feeling this way—was it right around the time you became the acting workflow lead, or had you been feeling off before that promotion happened?\n\nPatient: No, I was feeling fine before that. I'd say... within the first month or two of taking that position, I could just tell I wasn't handling work the way I normally do.\n\nDoctor: Apart from feeling drained at work, how has your overall mood been? It's more just that I'm so burnt out from the week that I don't have the energy for much else. So I just kind of sit around. But the bad mood really only hits when I'm dealing with work.\n\nDoctor: Is the acting workflow lead position still your current role, or has that changed since you started feeling this way?\n\nPatient: No, it's still my current role. The acting lead responsibilities are still in place. So, you know, I'm still dealing with this change every single day. I'd say this has all been going on for about four months now. Basically right since they merged the inventory teams and put me in this acting workflow lead role. It's just been an everyday thing since then.\n\nDoctor: You mentioned snapping at people over SAP errors—have your interactions with coworkers involved any behavior that you'd say crossed a line, like yelling at them, being disrespectful in a way that upset them, or acting in ways that violated workplace conduct expectations?\n\nPatient: No, I wouldn't say I've crossed a line like that.
